According to the proposed distance measure \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,\,\,OBPI_{N} } \right) = \frac{1}{n}\sum\limits_{i = 1}^{n} {\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right),\,\,\,OBPI_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right)} ,$$\end{document}dBPHOBPIM,OBPIN=1n∑i=1nH⌢OBPIMzi,OBPINzi, where \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) \le 1\,$$\end{document}0≤dBPHOBPIM,OBPIN≤1. So, axiom 1 is proved because of absolute values.

Axiom 2

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d\left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) = 0\,\,\,\,iff\,\,OBPI_{M} = OBPI_{N}$$\end{document}dOBPIM,OBPIN=0iffOBPIM=OBPIN.

Proof

If \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPI_{M} = OBPI_{N}$$\end{document}OBPIM=OBPIN then \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) = Bl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right)$$\end{document}BlMzi=BlNzi and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) = Pl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right)$$\end{document}PlMzi=PlNzi thus \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) = 0$$\end{document}dBPHOBPIM,OBPIN=0. Conversely, if \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) = 0,\,\,\forall \,z_{i} \in \overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{G}$$\end{document}dBPHOBPIM,OBPIN=0,∀zi∈G⌢. \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left| {Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right| = 0$$\end{document}BlMzi-BlNzi=0 and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\,\left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right| = 0\,\,\,\,$$\end{document}PlMzi-PlNzi=0, since \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) = Bl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right)\,\,$$\end{document}BlMzi=BlNzi and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) = Pl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right)$$\end{document}PlMzi=PlNzi. Thus, axiom 2 is satisfied.

Axiom 3

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d\left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) = d\left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{M} } \right)$$\end{document}dOBPIM,OBPIN=dOBPIN,OBPIM.

Proof

Since, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\max \left\{ {\left| {Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|,\left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|} \right\} = \max \left\{ {\left| {Bl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|,Pl\left| {_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|} \right\}$$\end{document}maxBlMzi-BlNzi,PlMzi-PlNzi=maxBlNzi-BlMzi,PlNzi-PlMzi, so, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) = \overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{M} } \right),\,\,\sum\limits_{i = 1}^{n} {\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right)} = \sum\limits_{i = 1}^{n} {\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{M} } \right)} ,$$\end{document}H⌢OBPIM,OBPIN=H⌢OBPIN,OBPIM,∑i=1nH⌢OBPIM,OBPIN=∑i=1nH⌢OBPIN,OBPIM, hence, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) = d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{M} } \right)$$\end{document}dBPHOBPIM,OBPIN=dBPHOBPIN,OBPIM. Thus axiom 3 is satisfied.

Axiom 4

If \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPI_{M} \le OBPI_{N} \, \le \,OBPI_{O} \,$$\end{document}OBPIM≤OBPIN≤OBPIO be any three orthoparian belief-plausibility intervals, then \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d\left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) \le d\left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{O} } \right)$$\end{document}dOBPIM,OBPIN≤dOBPIN,OBPIO, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d\left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) \le d\left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{O} } \right)$$\end{document}dOBPIM,OBPIO≤dOBPIN,OBPIO.

Proof

Now, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPI_{M} \le OBPI_{N} \, \le OBPI_{O}$$\end{document}OBPIM≤OBPIN≤OBPIO then, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) \le Bl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right) \le Bl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)$$\end{document}BlMzi≤BlNzi≤BlOzi and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) \ge Pl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right) \ge Pl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)$$\end{document}PlMzi≥PlNzi≥PlOzi thus, we have, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) = \max \left\{ {\left| {Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|,\left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|} \right\}$$\end{document}H⌢OBPIM,OBPIN=maxBlMzi-BlNzi,PlMzi-PlNzi and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) = \max \left\{ {\left| {Bl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|,\left| {Pl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|} \right\}$$\end{document}H⌢OBPIN,OBPIO=maxBlNzi-BlOzi,PlNzi-PlOzi hence \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) = \max \left\{ {\left| {Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|,\left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|} \right\}$$\end{document}H⌢OBPIM,OBPIO=maxBlMzi-BlOzi,PlMzi-PlOzi which implies that, if \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left| {Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right| \ge \left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|$$\end{document}BlMzi-BlOzi≥PlMzi-PlOzi then \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) = \left| {Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|$$\end{document}H⌢OBPIM,OBPIO=BlMzi-BlOzi but we have \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right| \le \left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right| \le \left| {Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|$$\end{document}PlMzi-PlNzi≤PlMzi-PlOzi≤BlMzi-BlOzi and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left| {Pl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right| \le \left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right| \le \left| {Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|\,.$$\end{document}PlNzi-PlOzi≤PlMzi-PlOzi≤BlMzi-BlOzi.

On the contrary we have,\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left| {Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right| \le \left| {Bl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|\,$$\end{document}BlMzi-BlNzi≤BlNzi-BlOzi and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left| {Bl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right| \le \left| {Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|$$\end{document}BlNzi-BlOzi≤BlMzi-BlOzi.

By combining we get, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) \le \overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right)$$\end{document}H⌢OBPIM,OBPIN≤H⌢OBPIM,OBPIO and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) \le \overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right)$$\end{document}H⌢OBPIN,OBPIO≤H⌢OBPIM,OBPIO which implies that, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) \le d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right)$$\end{document}dBPHOBPIM,OBPIN≤dBPHOBPIM,OBPIO and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) \le d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right)$$\end{document}dBPHOBPIN,OBPIO≤dBPHOBPIM,OBPIO.

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) \le \overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right)\,{\text{and}}\,\,\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) \le \overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right),$$\end{document}H⌢OBPIM,OBPIN≤H⌢OBPIM,OBPIOandH⌢OBPIN,OBPIO≤H⌢OBPIM,OBPIO, implies that \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) \le d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right)$$\end{document}dBPHOBPIM,OBPIN≤dBPHOBPIM,OBPIO. and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) \le d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right)$$\end{document}dBPHOBPIN,OBPIO≤dBPHOBPIM,OBPIO implies that, if \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left| {Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right| \le \left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|\,$$\end{document}BlMzi-BlOzi≤PlMzi-PlOzi then \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\,\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) = \left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|\,$$\end{document}H⌢OBPIM,OBPIO=PlMzi-PlOzi, but we have, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\,\left| {Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right| \le \left| {Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right| \le \left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|\,\,$$\end{document}BlMzi-BlNzi≤BlMzi-BlOzi≤PlMzi-PlOzi and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left| {Bl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right| \le \left| {Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right| \le \left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|$$\end{document}BlNzi-BlOzi≤BlMzi-BlOzi≤PlMzi-PlOzi. On the contrary we have, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right| \le \left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|\,\,$$\end{document}PlMzi-PlNzi≤PlMzi-PlOzi and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left| {Pl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right| \le \left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|$$\end{document}PlNzi-PlOzi≤PlMzi-PlOzi.

By combining we get \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) \le \overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right),$$\end{document}H⌢OBPIM,OBPIN≤H⌢OBPIM,OBPIO, and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\,\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) \le \overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right)$$\end{document}H⌢OBPIM,OBPIO≤H⌢OBPIM,OBPIO implies that, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) \le d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right)$$\end{document}dBPHOBPIM,OBPIN≤dBPHOBPIM,OBPIO and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) \le d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right)$$\end{document}dBPHOBPIN,OBPIO≤dBPHOBPIM,OBPIO. So, this proves the axiom 4.

Axiom 5

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$If\,\,OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} \,{\text{and}}\,OBPI_{O} \,$$\end{document}IfOBPIM,OBPINandOBPIO be any three orthoparian belief-plausibility intervals, then \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d\left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) \le d\left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) + d\left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{O} } \right)$$\end{document}dOBPIM,OBPIO≤dOBPIM,OBPIN+dOBPIN,OBPIO.

Proof

To prove the triangular inequality of \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left( {d_{5} } \right)$$\end{document}d5, let \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} \,$$\end{document}OBPIM,OBPIN and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPI_{O} \,$$\end{document}OBPIO be the three belief and plausibility intervals. If \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left| {Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right| \ge \left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|\,$$\end{document}BlMzi-BlOzi≥PlMzi-PlOzi then, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\,\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) = \left| {Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|$$\end{document}H⌢OBPIM,OBPIO=BlMzi-BlOzi. So, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) = \left| {Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right) + Bl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|.$$\end{document}H⌢OBPIM,OBPIO=BlMzi-BlNzi+BlNzi-BlOzi.

Thus, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) \le \left| {Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right| + \left| {Bl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|$$\end{document}H⌢OBPIM,OBPIO≤BlMzi-BlNzi+BlNzi-BlOzi and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) \le d\left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) + d\left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{O} } \right)$$\end{document}H⌢OBPIM,OBPIO≤dOBPIM,OBPIN+dOBPIN,OBPIO where, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) = max\left\{ {\left| {Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|,\left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|} \right\}$$\end{document}dBPHOBPIM,OBPIN=maxBlMzi-BlNzi,PlMzi-PlNzi and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) = max\left\{ {\left| {Bl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|,\left| {Pl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|} \right\}$$\end{document}dBPHOBPIN,OBPIO=maxBlNzi-BlOzi,PlNzi-PlOzi. Thus, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) \le \overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) + \overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{O} } \right)$$\end{document}H⌢OBPIM,OBPIO≤H⌢OBPIM,OBPIN+H⌢OBPIN,OBPIO implies that \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) \le d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) + d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{O} } \right).$$\end{document}dBPHOBPIM,OBPIO≤dBPHOBPIM,OBPIN+dBPHOBPIN,OBPIO.

If \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left| {Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right| \le \left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|$$\end{document}BlMzi-BlOzi≤PlMzi-PlOzi, then \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) = \left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|$$\end{document}H⌢OBPIM,OBPIO=PlMzi-PlOzi.

So, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) = \left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right) + Pl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|$$\end{document}H⌢OBPIM,OBPIO=PlMzi-PlNzi+PlNzi-PlOzi\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$ \overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) \le \left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right| + \left| {Pl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right| \le d\left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) + d\left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{O} } \right). $$\end{document}H⌢OBPIM,OBPIO≤PlMzi-PlNzi+PlNzi-PlOzi≤dOBPIM,OBPIN+dOBPIN,OBPIO.where \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d\left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) = max\left\{ {\left| {Bl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|,\left| {Pl_{M} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|} \right\}$$\end{document}dOBPIM,OBPIN=maxBlMzi-BlNzi,PlMzi-PlNzi and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d\left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) = max\left\{ {\left| {Bl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Bl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|,\left| {Pl_{N} \left( {z_{i} } \right) - Pl_{O} \left( {z_{i} } \right)} \right|} \right\}$$\end{document}dOBPIN,OBPIO=maxBlNzi-BlOzi,PlNzi-PlOzi. Hence, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) \le \overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) + \overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{O} } \right)$$\end{document}H⌢OBPIM,OBPIO≤H⌢OBPIM,OBPIN+H⌢OBPIN,OBPIO implies that, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{O} } \right) \le d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) + d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{O} } \right)$$\end{document}dBPHOBPIM,OBPIO≤dBPHOBPIM,OBPIN+dBPHOBPIN,OBPIO. Thus, all axioms are proved. □

Theorem 3

Consider \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPI_{M} \,$$\end{document}OBPIM and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPI_{N}$$\end{document}OBPIN are two belief-plausibility intervals on q-ROFSs in the universe of discourse \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\tilde{Z} = \left\{ {z_{1} ,z_{2} ,...,z_{n} } \right\}$$\end{document}Z~=z1,z2,...,zn then the proposed distance fulfills the following properties:

1. \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) = d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} \cap OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{M} \cup OBPI_{N} } \right)$$\end{document}dBPHOBPIM,OBPIN=dBPHOBPIM∩OBPIN,OBPIM∪OBPIN.

2. \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{M} \cap OBPI_{N} } \right) = d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{M} \cup OBPI_{N} } \right)$$\end{document}dBPHOBPIM,OBPIM∩OBPIN=dBPHOBPIN,OBPIM∪OBPIN.

3. \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{M} \cup OBPI_{N} } \right) = d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{N} ,OBPI_{M} \cap OBPI_{N} } \right)$$\end{document}dBPHOBPIM,OBPIM∪OBPIN=dBPHOBPIN,OBPIM∩OBPIN.

Similarity measures of q-ROFSs for belief and plausibility intervals

Similarity measures are very important tools to distinguish two different sets or objects. Similarity measure has numerous applications in many fields including pattern recognition, clustering and MCCDM22,53,54. Distance and similarity measures are commonly considered as dual concepts. So we can also find the similarity directly if the distance is given from the relation \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\text{similarity}}\,{ = 1 - }\,{\text{distance}}$$\end{document}similarity=1-distance. Hence, the Hausdorff distance between two \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPIs,$$\end{document}OBPIs,\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPI_{M}$$\end{document}OBPIM and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPI_{N}$$\end{document}OBPIN of q-ROFSs is used to define similarities between them. Let \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$f$$\end{document}f be a monotone decreasing function and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPI_{M}$$\end{document}OBPIM and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPI_{N}$$\end{document}OBPIN be two \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPIs$$\end{document}OBPIs. Since \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) \le 1,$$\end{document}0≤dBPHOBPIM,OBPIN≤1,\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$f\left( 1 \right) \le f\left( {d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right)} \right) \le f\left( 0 \right),$$\end{document}f1≤fdBPHOBPIM,OBPIN≤f0, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$f\left( 1 \right) \le f\left( {d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right)} \right) \le f\left( 0 \right)$$\end{document}f1≤fdBPHOBPIM,OBPIN≤f0 which implies that \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$f\left( 1 \right) \le f\left( {d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right)} \right) \le f\left( 0 \right)$$\end{document}f1≤fdBPHOBPIM,OBPIN≤f0. So, the similarity measure among \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPI_{M}$$\end{document}OBPIM and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPI_{N}$$\end{document}OBPIN will be,9\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$ S\left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) = \frac{{f\left( {d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right)} \right) - f\left( 1 \right)}}{f\left( 0 \right) - f\left( 1 \right)}\, $$\end{document}SOBPIM,OBPIN=fdBPHOBPIM,OBPIN-f1f0-f1

After selecting suitable functions, we get various similarity measures with the help of above Eq. (9). First we select the simple linear function \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$f\left( x \right) = 1 - x$$\end{document}fx=1-x. From Eq. (9), the similarity measure among \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPI_{M}$$\end{document}OBPIM and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPI_{N}$$\end{document}OBPIN of q-ROFSs will be.10\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$ S_{L} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) = 1 - d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right). $$\end{document}SLOBPIM,OBPIN=1-dBPHOBPIM,OBPIN.

Again, we select the simple function \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$f\left( x \right) = \frac{1}{1 + x}$$\end{document}fx=11+x so, the similarity measure among \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPI_{M}$$\end{document}OBPIM and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPI_{N}$$\end{document}OBPIN of q-ROFS can be defined as follows:11\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$ S_{Q} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) = \frac{{1 - d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right)}}{{1 + d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right)}}\, $$\end{document}SQOBPIM,OBPIN=1-dBPHOBPIM,OBPIN1+dBPHOBPIM,OBPIN

Also, we select the well-known function \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$f\left( x \right) = e^{ - x}$$\end{document}fx=e-x. So, the similarity measure between \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPI_{M}$$\end{document}OBPIM and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPI_{N}$$\end{document}OBPIN of q-ROFS is constructed as:12\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$ S_{E} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) = \frac{{e^{{ - d_{BPH} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right)}} - e^{ - 1} }}{{1 - e^{ - 1} }} $$\end{document}SEOBPIM,OBPIN=e-dBPHOBPIM,OBPIN-e-11-e-1

Now, some numerical examples are presented to test the validity of suggested similarities of Eqs. (10)–(12).

Example 2

Consider \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\tilde{Z} = \left\{ {z_{1} ,z_{2} ,z_{3} } \right\}\,$$\end{document}Z~=z1,z2,z3 be a universal set and let we have any two q-ROFSs as: \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$M = \left\{ {\left\langle {z_{1} ,0.87,0.65} \right\rangle \left\langle {z_{2} ,0.9,0.5} \right\rangle } \right\}\,\,{\text{and}}\,N = \left\{ {\left\langle {z_{1} ,0.6,0.9} \right\rangle \left\langle {z_{2} ,0.81,0.7} \right\rangle } \right\}$$\end{document}M=z1,0.87,0.65z2,0.9,0.5andN=z1,0.6,0.9z2,0.81,0.7. We have to solve proposed similarity measure between belief-plausibility of q-ROFSs.\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$ OBPI_{M} = \left\{ {\left\langle {z_{1} ,\left[ {0.658,0.725} \right]} \right\rangle ,\left\langle {z_{2} ,\left[ {0.729,0.125} \right]} \right\rangle } \right\} $$\end{document}OBPIM=z1,0.658,0.725,z2,0.729,0.125

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$OBPI_{N} = \left\{ {\left\langle {z_{1} ,\left[ {0.216,0.729} \right]} \right\rangle ,\left\langle {z_{2} ,\left[ {0.531,0.343} \right]} \right\rangle } \right\}$$\end{document}OBPIN=z1,0.216,0.729,z2,0.531,0.343,\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$ \overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) = \left\{ {\left\langle {z_{1} ,0.442} \right\rangle ,\left\langle {z_{2} ,0.218} \right\rangle } \right\} $$\end{document}H⌢OBPIM,OBPIN=z1,0.442,z2,0.218

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$d\left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) = \frac{1}{n}\sum\limits_{i = 1}^{n} {\overset{\lower0.5em\hbox{$\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\frown}$}}{H} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right)} = 0.333$$\end{document}dOBPIM,OBPIN=1n∑i=1nH⌢OBPIM,OBPIN=0.333.

By using Eq. (10)–(12), we have,

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$S_{L} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) = 0.667$$\end{document}SLOBPIM,OBPIN=0.667; \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$S_{Q} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) = 0.5003$$\end{document}SQOBPIM,OBPIN=0.5003; \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$S_{E} \left( {OBPI_{M} ,OBPI_{N} } \right) = 0.5518$$\end{document}SEOBPIM,OBPIN=0.5518.

Application in pattern recognition

Pattern recognition focuses on how computer programs can recognize various patterns. It is used to classify or categorize these patterns and make judgments regarding their classes. Due to its expanding relevance in several domains, this has been a major and compelling study topic for almost five decades. We provide an example of pattern recognition by employing the proposed similarities of Eqs. (10)–(12) below.

Application in clustering

Clustering is a type of unsupervised machine learning technique that involves grouping together data points based on their similarity. Clustering algorithms are used to partition a set of data points into clusters, where the points within each cluster are more similar to each other than they are to points in other clusters. Now, we present the application of proposed similarity measure Eq. (10) in clustering. The steps towards clustering are given as follows:

Choose the function f using Eq. (10) and analyze \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$S\left( {OBPI_{{M_{i} }} ,OBPI_{{M_{j} }} } \right)$$\end{document}SOBPIMi,OBPIMj and develop the Pythagorean similarity matrix.

(i) The satisfaction level is denoted by \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\,\tau \in \left[ {0,1} \right]$$\end{document}τ∈0,1.

Let \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\zeta_{ij}^{\tau } = \,\left\{ \begin{gathered} 1,\,\,S\left( {OBPI_{{M_{i} }} ,OBPI_{{M_{j} }} } \right)\,\, \ge \,\,\,\tau \hfill \\ 0,\,\,S\left( {OBPI_{{M_{i} }} ,OBPI_{{M_{j} }} } \right)\, < \,\,\tau \hfill \\ \end{gathered} \right.,$$\end{document}ζijτ=1,SOBPIMi,OBPIMj≥τ0,SOBPIMi,OBPIMj<τ, and then develop the \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\tau$$\end{document}τ-cutting matrix \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$D_{\tau } = \left( {\zeta_{ij}^{\tau } } \right)_{n \times n}$$\end{document}Dτ=ζijτn×n.

(ii) Obtain the corresponding clustering result based on \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$D_{\tau } = \left( {\zeta_{ij}^{\tau } } \right)_{n \times n}$$\end{document}Dτ=ζijτn×n.

Example 4

$$\end{document}M2=z1,0.9,0.5,z2,0.7,0.3,z3,0.8,0.4,z4,0.6,0.7;\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$ M_{3} = \left\{ {\left( {z_{1} ,\,0.9,\,\,0.4} \right),\left( {z_{2} ,0.8,\,\,0.3} \right),\left( {z_{3} ,0.6,\,\,0.7} \right),\left( {z_{4} ,0.8,\,\,0.6} \right)} \right\}; So from the real clustering processes, the decision-makers can choose the suitable function \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$f$$\end{document}f regarding the actual situations.
